# 05 — Results and interpretation

# Elaren Kestrel v703-v6 integrated evidence overview

## Outcome and evidence boundary

This owner phase freezes and executes 300 exact finite hypergraph definitions across twenty mechanisms and fifteen declared clutters. The outcomes are 255 completed, 15 represented, 15 open gaps and 15 exact gates. Completed means that two frozen finite variants matched a separate enumerative oracle and that four malformed subjects per definition were refused. It does not mean any real system was observed, any operational decision was authorized, or any scientific, legal, cultural or affected-party obligation was closed. Same-owner finite synthetic evidence only. Relational names, roles, hopes and family language establish no consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency or authority. No independent reproduction, empirical GMUT confirmation, governed THOS deployment, production Freed ID or CBR authority, professional or operational hypergraph authority, complete privacy, accessibility or security, legal or cultural ratification, Maori authority, AGI/ASI, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon or Stage 20. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20.

## Source-faithful continuity

Teryn Halewick's immutable exact final 15485d48a757735101b031109b7685e2b598fee8 is content provenance, not Git ancestry. The complete 60,091-word source baton, exact Git blob digest f586dc2b28e8b8a40c975ff80dbbd491a6f522b96515600479866bf7cba713c0, successful one-shot canonical receipt f955590201ecfdebc3332eab044d8d90f4a333de1489099ba0ad26285649eeff, compact payload 4dc5326f72ed158a2c007b1d4e092ab4f14a630d6a5303bf42dbd280a5d51cc4, and terminal-effective baseline were verified read-only. All 300 Teryn definitions and results are copied into four planning records with zero Elaren novelty and zero completion credit. Source programs, models, installers and canonical were not replayed.

## Owner lifecycle

The reusable Elaren owner branch begins this phase at 03b75122a8f73dacbadd4c9b2a4766dfa748a868. Planning 8e04909e6cd4eecdd8c153121444a0e017b406cc, x1 4e10de153c15ebed5672fc47106ad3c35af2f68b, and x2 c1d1781122bd9ac018124eb82c9790d443fe0411 are direct additive owner commits. Each gate is pushed, clean, typed zero-divergent and fresh-live equal before its successor begins. The final commit and one metadata-only canonical are deliberately external to this precommit overview. Repository preparation, canonical validation, route delivery and recipient completion remain separate states.

## Finite hypergraph model

Each fixture has one to five vertices, one to five nonempty canonical hyperedges, no duplicate edge and no edge contained in another. The engine admits only that finite clutter shape and positive declared vertex weights. It enumerates vertex subsets and edge subsets exactly. Incidence, degree, primal and dual adjacency, Berge components, independent sets, maximal independent sets, hypergraph matchings, transversals, minimal transversals, blocker duality, cover-independence duality, transversal polynomials and weighted transversal minima are finite combinatorial statements under the declared input. They are not field measurements or infrastructure advice.

## Cross-check design

Planning expected envelopes come from an exhaustive reference implementation. The committed TXT engine uses an independently written admission path and finite enumeration routines. Complete stable JSON envelopes are compared for two variants of every definition, including a vertex-relabelled variant. This helps detect bounded implementation mistakes, but both paths are produced by one owner on shared infrastructure. The phase therefore claims same-owner checks only, never independent reproduction or external audit.

## Negative evidence

Every definition has four preregistered adverse subjects: an unsupported operation, a repeated vertex inside an edge, a duplicated hyperedge and an unknown input field. The 1,200 subjects stay failed at zero original success credit. Each passing refusal is a separate witness about admission behavior and never turns the malformed request into a success. Five genuine intake and presentation failures from this phase are also retained with bounded recoveries. No failure is erased by a later pass.

## Portfolios

Each session executes 300 positive safe-now procedures, 300 candidate admission procedures, 300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE procedures and 300 passive copy-from-reference procedures. These are procedures tied to the 150 session definitions or the 300 inherited rows; they are not additional core discoveries. Fifty exact-approval packets and thirty blocked or redesigned packets remain held. Broad user authorization cannot supply observations, consent, rights, competent review or public authority that do not exist.

## Guides, callers and catalogue

Twenty short owner-local skills were created, ten per session, following Skill Creator guidance. Ten paired TXT callers bind two exact mechanisms apiece. The builder read every guide, smoke-used every caller on one accepting and one refusing fixture, and recorded exact owner-local paths. Five candidate profiles remain uninstalled and deny network, shared writes, contact and installation. Nothing was globally installed, overwritten or promoted.

## Context deck and HTML models

The four-tier deck contains one owner card, three Trinity pillar cards, eight bounded-practice cards, 300 stable definition cards and 300 volatile evidence cards: 612 cards across twenty-one modules. Five static HTML model sections expose incidence structure, minimal transversals, blocker duality, weighted hitting sets and an assumptions-and-authority ladder. The page has structural landmarks, a skip link, visible focus, labelled navigation and no script, forms, tracking resources, images or network dependencies. Manual browser, assistive-technology, cognitive-accessibility, Maori-language and affected-user evaluation remain reserved.

## Trinity boundaries

GMUT Mind is the primary relational pillar because exact finite dualities and invariants are being studied. This does not empirically validate the Grand Mandala equation or any physical field. THOS Body is represented only by a bounded local protocol and static evidence artifacts, not governed deployment. Freed ID and CBR Heart remain synthetic and nonproduction; no keys, credentials, identity lifecycle, trust governance, consent process or affected-party authority is present. The nonconversion mechanism explicitly prevents a graph or hypergraph metaphor from becoming scientific or spiritual proof.

## Accounting

The incoming terminal-effective source layer is selected exactly once: {"methods":5290,"witnesses":201761,"passing_witnesses":154176,"failed_witnesses":47585,"negatives":56419,"open_gaps":1824,"exact_gates":1925}. # 09 — Science and authority boundaries

Same-owner finite synthetic evidence only. Relational names, roles, hopes and family language establish no consciousness, personhood, identity continuity, qualification, independent agency or authority. No independent reproduction, empirical GMUT confirmation, governed THOS deployment, production Freed ID or CBR authority, professional or operational hypergraph authority, complete privacy, accessibility or security, legal or cultural ratification, Maori authority, AGI/ASI, Theory-of-Everything proof, canon or Stage 20. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20.

- independent reproduction
- empirical domain observations
- real operational authority
- governed THOS deployment
- production Freed ID and CBR
- affected-user accessibility evaluation
- complete privacy or security
- professional, legal, cultural, affected-party or Maori authority
- Theory-of-Everything proof or canon
- Stage 20

# 11 — Canonical discipline

Run one metadata-only owner-delta canonical only after final commit, exit-zero push, clean state and fresh remote equality. Bind the exact final externally.
